| P1G-021 | Fix — click routes to the correct table cell/column + no-cache dev server | Claude Code | In review | **Bug (pre-existing, exposed by table editing):** clicking any cell in a table row placed the caret in **column 1**. `hit_test` chose the target line by **vertical band only** (`find(contains_y)`), and every cell in a row shares that band, so it always took the leftmost. Not a table-work regression — `git log` shows `hittest.rs` untouched since a viewer fix well before the table commits; cell editing simply made it visible. **Fix (`casual-doc-layout/hittest.rs`):** `LineBox` now carries the cell's page-local x-range (`CellFragment.x`/`width`; innermost cell for nested tables; `None` for body paragraphs), and `hit_test` selects the line by band **and** cell — the line whose cell contains x, else the horizontally-nearest cell, else the first (single-column paragraphs unchanged, since a body line matches every x). The empty-space branch is column-aware too. **Verified:** unit test (a 2-column row resolves a click in each column to that column's node) + in-browser (clicking "Nested B" lands in column 2 — node `…0024` vs column 1's `…0021` — and typing inserts there). **Also:** `webapp/serve.py` — a **no-cache** dev server (the default `http.server` let the browser heuristically cache the wasm/JS, silently serving stale builds — the "I updated it but nothing changed" trap); `build.sh` now points at it. All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--all-targets --all-features --locked`, layout tests). |
| P1G-020 | Tables — column insert/delete (regular tables) | Claude Code | In review | Continues [[P1G-019]]: structural **column** editing. `casual-doc-edit`: additive ops `InsertColumn { table, index, width, cells }` / `DeleteColumn { table, index }` (inverses; `DeleteColumn` refuses a table's only column, its inverse carries the removed grid width + one cell per row for exact undo), plus `locate_table_cell(node) -> (table_id, cell_index)`. A shared guard `ensure_regular_table` restricts column edits to **regular** tables — non-empty grid, every row exactly one cell per grid column, no `gridSpan`/`vMerge` — so a merged table is refused (`Unsupported`), never corrupted; spanned/merged column edits are a later slice. WASM: `insertColumn(node, after)` adds a grid column + one empty cell to every row (fresh ids, width copied from the anchor column), caret landing in the new cell of the caret's row; `deleteColumn(node)` removes the caret's column across all rows, caret landing in an adjacent surviving cell; both reuse `inTable` gating. `webapp/`: Insert-column / Delete-column buttons (Material SVG) beside the row buttons, enabled only in a table. 1 wasm test on the fixture's regular nested table (grid + per-row cell counts grow/shrink together, new cell editable, both ops undo). Same **in-browser verification caveat** as P1G-019 — automation input to the page was unavailable this session, so the buttons are unit-verified but not click-verified. All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). **Deferred:** merged/spanned-cell column edits, merge/split cells, delete whole table. |
| P1G-019 | Tables — in-cell editing (verified) + structural row insert/delete | Claude Code | In review | First **tables** slice. **Finding:** in-cell **text** editing already works end-to-end — hit-testing resolves a point in a cell to that cell's paragraph (`collect_fragment` recurses into `TableRow`/cells), caret geometry resolves, and every edit op (insert/delete/format) walks tables via `find_paragraph_mut`. Locked by a wasm test: caret rect, insert, copy, bold, and delete all operate on the nested-table cell paragraph "Nested A". **New — structural row editing.** `casual-doc-edit`: additive ops `InsertRow { table, index, row }` / `DeleteRow { table, index }` (inverses of each other; `DeleteRow` refuses a table's last row since rows are non-empty, and its inverse carries the removed row for exact undo), plus `find_table` (read-only) and `locate_table_row(node) -> (table_id, row_index, cloned_row)`. WASM: `insertRow(node, below)` builds an empty row mirroring the anchor row's column structure (cell count / widths / spans preserved, one empty paragraph per cell, fresh ids from the edit namespace) and inserts it above/below, caret landing in the new row; `deleteRow(node)` removes the caret's row, caret landing in an adjacent surviving row; `inTable(node)` gates the toolbar. `webapp/`: Insert-row / Delete-row buttons (Material SVG), enabled only inside a table. 3 wasm tests (cell edit+caret; row insert/delete round-trip with new-row editability + undo). **Verification note:** engine paths are unit-tested; live in-browser button verification was **blocked this session** — the automation stopped delivering pointer *and* keyboard events to the page mid-run (screenshots/JS still worked; the same clicks drove the earlier list/bold/Tab checks), so the row buttons could not be exercised in the browser. All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). **Deferred:** insert/delete **column**, delete whole table, merge/split cells, and the cross-table structural text delete. |
| P1G-018 | Lists — bullet / numbered toggle | Claude Code | In review | The last big paragraph capability. **Model:** additive `Document::definitions_mut()` — the seam an editor uses to register document-level infrastructure an edit references (body edits stay on the closed op set; this is for the side-tables they point at). **WASM:** `toggleList(range, "bullet"|"numbered")` — lazily creates one shared `AbstractNumbering`+`NumberingInstance` per list kind (ids from the edit namespace; reused after, so the document grows at most one definition per kind), then toggles each selected paragraph's `numbering` ref via the existing undoable `SetParagraphProperties`/`apply_paragraph_props` path. The definition is infrastructure (an unreferenced instance is valid), so it is not part of undo; undo removes the paragraph's ref. Direction is decided by the first selected paragraph (on iff it is not already this list). Bullet level = `•` glyph, numbered = `%1.` decimal, both with a 0.5″ indent / 0.25″ hanging marker; the flow engine's `NumberingState` renders and advances the markers. `listStyleAt(node)` → `"bullet"`/`"numbered"`/`""` (also resolves an imported list's format) drives the toolbar. **`webapp/`:** bullet + numbered buttons (Material SVG), active-state reflection, disabled without a selection. **Enter continues the list automatically** (`splitParagraph` copies the paragraph properties, so the new paragraph keeps the numbering) — verified. **Verified in-browser:** bullet renders `• After the nested table.`; numbered switches it to `1.`; Enter + type yields `2. Second item` (counter advances). 1 wasm test (toggle reflects, definition created once + reused, undo removes, numbering survives export → re-open). All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). **Deferred:** multi-level demote/promote (Tab inside a list changes indent, not level yet), restart-numbering, and choosing the marker format/style. |
| P1G-017 | Format-run coalescing + Tab/Shift+Tab indent | Claude Code | In review | Paragraph polish + a latent-correctness fix. **(1) `FormatText` now coalesces** adjacent equal-property runs, the same invariant [[P1G-016]] handled for delete: formatting a sub-range to *match* its neighbour (e.g. bolding text next to already-bold text) produced two adjacent equal-property runs, which the model forbids — a doc that would re-validate/export dirty. The inverse is already `SetInlines`, so undo still restores the exact original run structure. **(2) Tab / Shift+Tab indent / outdent** the paragraph(s) the selection touches (±360 twips via the existing `adjustIndent`), the word-processor convention and the basis for list demote/promote; caught in `webapp/` before the browser moves focus, and it disarms any pending typing-format. **Verified in-browser:** Tab indents "Paragraph with an image:" by 0.25″. 1 edit-crate test (format-to-match-neighbour coalesces + stays valid + undo restores the split). All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). |
| P1G-016 | Multi-run / cross-paragraph delete + typing with an armed format | Claude Code | In review | Two blocking editor gaps the owner hit. **(1) Multi-paragraph select + Backspace/Delete was a no-op** ("edit ignored: Unsupported"). Root cause: `delete_text` only handled a range **within one run**, but a real formatted paragraph has many runs, so a selection's tail/head (what a cross-paragraph delete reduces to per paragraph) spanned runs → `Unsupported` → the atomic batch rolled back. Fix (`casual-doc-edit`): `DeleteText` keeps the cheap single-run fast path (plain `InsertText` inverse) but adds a general path — snapshot, `ensure_run_boundary` at both ends, drop every fully-covered inline (`remove_covered_range`), and **coalesce** adjacent equal-property runs (the model forbids them, and a delete between two such runs would create them). The inverse is `SetInlines`, so undo restores each deleted run's own formatting (a plain-text inverse could not). A partial cut into a non-splittable nested wrapper (hyperlink/SDT) is refused, not corrupted. **This also fixes ⌘X cut and ⌘V paste over multi-run selections** (both route through `deleteSelection`). **(2) Couldn't type in a bold/italic state.** New `casual-doc-edit::caret_format` (the run format a caret inherits — Word's "run to the left" rule); WASM `caretFormat` + `insertStyledText` (atomic `InsertText`+`FormatText`, caret pinned after the text, consecutive armed typing coalesces into one run); `webapp/` arms B/I/U/S at a collapsed caret (enabled + pressed to reflect the armed/inherited format) and types through `insertStyledText`, disarming on any caret move (click / arrow / doc-nav). **Verified in-browser:** armed Bold → typed "BOLD" renders bold; a cross-paragraph selection + Backspace merges the paragraphs with no Unsupported. 4 edit-crate + 2 wasm tests (multi-run delete + undo-restores-formatting, coalesce-stays-valid, full-para delete, styled-insert types+undoes, delete-across-runs succeeds+undoes). All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--all-targets --all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). |
| P1G-014 | Reflect-state toolbar + backward/upward selection highlight | Claude Code | In review | Two premium-editor behaviors. **(1) Backward/upward selection now highlights.** `selectionRects` used `parse_range` (no ordering), so an inverted range — dragging right-to-left / bottom-to-top — produced no rectangles ("drag works downward but not upward"); it now orders the endpoints in document order first, like the other range methods. Verified in-browser: same-line and cross-paragraph **backward** drags highlight (6 rects across two paragraphs). **(2) Toolbar reflects the selection, not just sets it.** `casual-doc-edit` `run_style_state(doc, range)` returns the uniform size / RGB color / font family / super-sub across the covered runs (blank when mixed); WASM `selectionRunStyle`/`lineSpacingAt`; `webapp/` `updateToolbar` sets the Size / Font / color / super-sub / Spacing controls to the selection's current values (cleared for a mixed / cross-paragraph selection). PR #180. All gates green (fmt 1.96.0, clippy `--all-features --locked`, workspace test, wasm). |
| P1G-015 | Incremental re-pagination — O(edit) not O(document) | Claude Code | In review | **Real-doc edit latency.** P1G-007 made the *repaint* incremental (only dirty pages re-raster) and reported ~10ms — but that measured only the DOM/raster path; underneath, `finish_edit` still called `paginate_document`, which **re-shapes every paragraph** (`build_galley_for_blocks`). Shaping is ~99% of layout cost, so per-edit latency stayed O(document): a 46-page doc cost **~55ms/edit** in WASM (the "100–150ms on real docs" report). The engine already had the incremental machinery (`GalleyCache`/`build_galley_cached`, P1D-004c) but it was never wired into the edit path. **`casual-doc-layout`:** new `paginate_document_cached(document, shaper, cache, dirty)` — incremental twin of `paginate_document`. For the common single-section body it builds the galley through the cache (unchanged paragraphs reuse their shaped lines; only paragraphs whose content **hash** changed re-shape); section/column-break docs fall back to a full re-shape. Both funnel through a shared `finish_pagination` tail so every post-pass runs identically and the output is **byte-for-byte the same**. Numbered / text-box paragraphs already bypass the cache (no stale list markers). **`casual-doc-wasm`:** `WasmDocument` holds a `GalleyCache`; `finish_edit` re-paginates through it; a font registration clears it; hash-based invalidation makes an empty dirty set correct. **Measured in-browser (WASM, 46-page doc): 55.5ms → 2.5ms/edit median (22×, under the 10ms target)**; first edit after open warms the cache once (~55ms). Native release (`examples/edit_latency.rs`): 2000-para/91-page 38.3ms → 3.4ms (11×), of which 37.0ms was the removed re-shape. 2 tests: cached pagination equals a full re-shape after a paragraph **edit** and after an **insert**, each re-shaping exactly one paragraph (correctness + incrementality). Dev examples: `edit_latency`, `gen_big_docx`. PR #181. All gates green. |
| P1G-012 | Premium editor UX — navigation, select-all, cut/paste, triple-click + SVG icons | Claude Code | In review | Editor-grade interaction + behavior audit. **Navigation** (WASM `moveCaret` extended + `firstPosition`/`lastPosition`/`paragraphLength`): Home/End (line, via caret-rect+hit-test probe), ⌘←/→ (line), ⌥←/→ (word, Unicode word boundaries), ⌘↑/↓ (document), all with **Shift** to extend. **Selection:** ⌘A select-all (whole document incl. table cells), **triple-click** selects the paragraph, double-click word + Shift+Click extend (existing). **Clipboard:** ⌘X cut (copy + delete) and ⌘V paste (clipboard text at caret, replacing the selection, newlines → paragraph splits). **Icons:** the toolbar's B/I/U/S/super-sub/align/indent labels replaced with **Material Symbols SVG** (Apache-2.0, inlined; PR #178). **Verified in-browser:** ⌘A highlights the entire document; Home moves the caret from mid-line to the left margin (196→76px). All gates green (clippy `--all-features --locked`). **Deferred:** cross-structure selection delete (Backspace across a table). |
| P1G-011 | Paragraph — font family + style (Heading/Title) — completes the element | Claude Code | In review | Finishes the paragraph. **`casual-doc-edit`:** `FormatDelta` gains `font` (sets `w:rFonts` ascii+hAnsi to a named family); `FormatDelta` is now `Clone` (String field). **WASM:** `setFont` (run), `setParagraphStyle`/`listStyles`/`paragraphStyleAt` (paragraph style via `style_ref` to an existing `StyleKind::Paragraph` style by name). **`webapp/`:** **Font** + **Style** dropdowns added to the toolbar; the Style dropdown is populated from the document's paragraph styles on open and reflects the caret's current style. **Verified in-browser:** applying **Heading 1** transforms a body paragraph into a large bold heading; Style dropdown lists the doc's styles (Heading 1, Normal, …). 1 wasm test (font applies; style applies/reads-back/undoes). **CI note:** run clippy with `--all-features --locked` locally (CI does) — caught `too_many_arguments` + redundant `.clone()` on `Copy` `Option<_>` that a plain local run missed. All gates green. **Remaining paragraph polish (deferred):** toolbar reflecting current size/color/spacing/font; cross-**structure** selection delete (Backspace across a table) — a separate structural-delete op. |
| P1G-010 | Paragraph — complete editing (paragraph + run properties + full toolbar) | Claude Code | In review | Going deep on the **paragraph element** per the production-grade "one element at a time, complete" directive. **`casual-doc-edit`:** additive `Operation::SetParagraphProperties{node, Box<ParagraphProperties>}` (own inverse via previous properties, exact undo) + `paragraph_properties` query; `FormatDelta` extended with `color`/`highlight`/`size_half_points`/`vertical_alignment` (run props). **WASM:** paragraph — `setAlignment`, `setLineSpacing` (100/150/200%), `adjustIndent` (±twips), `setParagraphShading`, `alignmentAt`; run — `setTextColor` (RGB), `setHighlight` (named), `setFontSize` (points), `setVertAlign` (super/sub/baseline). All apply across every paragraph/sub-range the selection touches, one undoable action, selection preserved (no collapse), incremental repaint. **`webapp/`:** a real toolbar — B/I/U/**S**, super/subscript, **font size**, **text color**, **highlight**, **align L/C/R/J**, **line spacing**, **indent ±** — with active states (bold/italic/underline/strike + alignment) and enable/disable by selection kind (run controls need a range, paragraph controls a caret). **Verified in-browser:** heading **centered**; a word set to **24pt**, **red**, and **yellow highlight**, each rendered correctly. 1 wasm test (alignment query + all setters apply/undo, text intact). All gates green. **Deferred (next):** font-family + paragraph **style** (Heading/Title) dropdowns; toolbar reflecting current size/color/spacing; the Carlito-for-Calibri viewer-fidelity option. |
| P1G-009 | Save (edited → .docx) + cross-paragraph formatting + dedicated toolbar | Claude Code | In review | **Closes the edit loop.** WASM `exportDocx()` serializes the edited `v1::Document` via the Phase-1B semantic writer (`casual_doc_export::write_document`) to a `.docx` package; `webapp/` **Save .docx** button downloads it (media served from a shared `BTreeMap` via a borrowing `MediaSource`). **Cross-paragraph formatting:** `formatSelection`/`selectionFormat` apply/query run-property toggles across a multi-paragraph selection (one `FormatText` per covered paragraph, one undoable action; query ANDs the per-paragraph states) — no longer single-paragraph-only. **UX:** the B/I/U formatting controls moved out of the header into a **dedicated toolbar row**. **Verified in-browser:** cross-paragraph ⌘B bolds a body word spanning from the heading; Save exports and downloads the edited doc. 1 wasm export round-trip test (edit→export→re-open) + 1 cross-paragraph formatting test (13 wasm total). | P1G-008 | Editing slice 3: run-property formatting (bold/italic/underline) + toolbar | Claude Code | In review | First **formatting** on `v1::Document` (doc 59 slice 3). **`casual-doc-edit`:** additive `Operation::FormatText{range, FormatDelta{bold,italic,underline,strike}}` — splits runs straddling the range (via `ensure_run_boundary`, fresh id for the tail) then sets the toggles on the covered runs; its inverse is a new `Operation::SetInlines{node, inlines}` that restores the paragraph's inlines verbatim, so undo is exact even after run-splits. `pub fn format_state(doc, range) -> FormatState` reports whether every covered run sets each toggle (toolbar active state + toggle direction). **WASM:** `formatText(node,start,end, bold?,italic?,underline?,strike?)` (tri-state args) + `formatAt(node,start,end) -> Format`; formatting keeps the selection (does not collapse). **`webapp/`:** a B/I/U toolbar (aria-pressed active state, disabled without a range) + ⌘B/⌘I/⌘U; toolbar buttons use mousedown+preventDefault so they never steal the selection; formatting repaints only dirty pages and preserves the selection. **Verified in-browser:** drag-select a word → ⌘B renders it bold (run split at the boundary, rest unchanged), toolbar shows pressed, ⌘Z restores. 1 edit-crate + 1 wasm formatting test. All gates green. **Deferred:** cross-paragraph formatting, color/size/font, collapsed-caret "typing format", the run-coalescing pass. |
| P1G-007 | Editing slice 2: structural edits + navigation + incremental repaint | Claude Code | In review | Makes editing feel complete (doc 59 slice 2, prioritized). **`casual-doc-edit`:** `SplitParagraph` (Enter) + `JoinParagraphs` (Backspace at ¶ start / Delete at ¶ end) with split↔join inverses; block-level container walk; a straddling run is split (fresh id). **Group undo:** `WasmDocument` undo/redo stacks hold op *groups*, so a cross-paragraph delete or type-over is one undoable action; multi-op batches are atomic (snapshot + rollback on failure — a cross-container selection reports Unsupported without corrupting), single-op typing stays clone-free. **WASM:** `splitParagraph`, `deleteSelection` (same- or cross-paragraph = delete ends + whole middles + join), `replaceSelection` (type-over), `moveCaret(dir)` (left/right by char across ¶; up/down via `LayoutSnapshot::move_vertical` across lines/pages), `wordAt` (Unicode word segmentation), and `Caret`. **Incremental repaint:** `EditResult` now carries `pageCount` + `dirtyPages` (indices whose `Page` differs old→new); the frontend re-rasters only those pages, reusing canvases — **edit latency ~100–150ms → ~10ms** (page raster measured 8.3ms, edit 1ms; the old cost was the full-DOM `renderAll`). **`webapp/`:** Enter split, arrow nav (+ `preventDefault` so up/down no longer scrolls), Shift+Arrow extend, Shift+Click extend, double-click word select, cross-paragraph delete, caret scrolls into view. Caret color fixed to black earlier (#168). **Verified in-browser:** arrows move caret (no scroll), Enter splits & reflows, undo rejoins, double-click selects the word, Shift+Click spans paragraphs, cross-¶ delete merges. 3 edit-crate split/join tests + 1 wasm split/word/move test. All gates green. **Deferred:** position-map selection remap, `subscribe`/`drainEvents`, nested-wrapper edits, run-property + object/table ops, IME. |
| P1F-53 | Per-section page/margin/column geometry for floating objects | Codex | Done | Merged in PR #158. Design: `docs/53-PER-SECTION-ANCHOR-GEOMETRY-DESIGN.md`. Body float collection now mirrors the paginator's section partition, page/margin/physical-side frames derive from the source `SectionBoundary`, the located top-level fragment supplies the actual `column` frame through nested tables, and repeated header/footer floats use the section recorded on their page. Final-rectangle tests cover a differently sized next-page section, two continuous sections sharing one page with different margins, a column-relative float in a nested table cell, a later-section header float, and top/bottom margin strips. Formatting, strict all-target/all-feature Clippy, full all-feature workspace tests, rustdoc with warnings denied, `wasm32-unknown-unknown`, Rust 1.88 MSRV, release benchmark smoke, and `git diff --check` passed. Exact character anchoring, mirrored inside/outside parity, per-section running-content selection/measurement, percentage offsets, and side wrapping remain explicit follow-ups. |
